We propose a paired weight-delta path audit and apply it to two public, aligned generalist-to-medical-specialist checkpoint pairs: Gemma-3-4B-IT to MedGemma-4B-IT and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct to HuatuoGPT-o1-7B. In both pairs, the full decoder-side update strongly reconstructs measured medical benchmark movement (0.974 and 1.183 endpoint-normalized retention), making each decoder delta an appropriate substrate for the audit. Yet the movement is not cleanly localized. MLP is the strongest broad component family in both pairs, but mixed off-domain movements, 10-seed matched controls, and endpoint-anchored rollbacks prevent a unique coarse-family explanation. The audit therefore separates update-level reconstruction from component-level explanation. Its claims concern text-only multiple-choice benchmark movement, not clinical validation, repair, or circuit-level mechanism.

</details>


### [250] [CAS: Conformalized Agentic Search via Adaptive Retrieval and Policy Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20771)
*Zixi Zhu,Jiayuan Su,Jian Zhang,Yu Lin,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对搜索智能体在强化学习微调过程中面临的检索可靠性危机（Top-K启发式检索导致关键证据丢失或噪声混入，以及过度自信导致的幻觉回答和冗余搜索），本文提出Conformalized Agentic Search (CAS)框架：检索侧利用Adaptive Prediction Set (APS)将统计覆盖转化为动态文档截断，构建大小自适应的预测集；训练侧利用Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI)动态构建有可控覆盖率的预测集以量化答案置信度，并在GRPO目标中惩罚低置信度轨迹。实验表明该方法在单跳和多跳QA数据集上显著提升推理准确率并大幅减少冗余工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索智能体在RL微调期间存在严重的可靠性危机：启发式Top-K检索会导致关键证据丢失或噪声引入；RL进程中累积的过度自信会导致幻觉答案和冗余搜索。现有方法无法为检索和训练两端提供可验证的可靠性保障。

Method: 提出Conformalized Agentic Search (CAS)，将Conformal Prediction (CP)引入智能体检索与训练：(1) 检索侧采用Adaptive Prediction Set (APS)，把统计覆盖率映射为动态文档截断，形成大小自适应的预测集；(2) 训练侧采用Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI)动态构建具有可控覆盖率的预测集来量化答案置信度，并将该置信度用于惩罚GRPO目标中的低置信度轨迹，确保模型只从可靠样本中学习。

Result: 在单跳和多跳问答数据集上的实验表明，CAS显著提升了推理准确率，同时大幅减少冗余工具调用次数，建立了高可靠且高效的智能体范式；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过在检索侧（自适应预测集）和训练侧（置信度感知的GRPO惩罚）同时建立可靠保障，CP可以系统性地缓解搜索智能体的证据丢失、噪声混入、幻觉和冗余搜索问题，为构建高可靠性的agent范式提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Search Agents face a severe reliability crisis during reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning. Heuristic Top-K retrieval often causes critical evidence loss or noise inclusion, while over-confidence induced by progressive RL leads to hallucinated answers and redundant searches.
  To build highly reliable agents, we introduce Conformal Prediction (CP) and propose Conformalized Agentic Search (CAS). This framework establishes reliability guarantees on both the retrieval and training sides: on the retrieval side, an Adaptive Prediction Set (APS), a specific CP realization, translates statistical coverage into dynamic document truncation to construct prediction sets that are adaptive in size; on the training side, Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI), a dynamic CP algorithm, dynamically constructs prediction sets with controllable coverage to quantify answer confidence, which is then used to penalize low-confidence trajectories within the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) objective, ensuring the model learns only from reliable ones.
  Experiments across single-hop and multi-hop QA datasets demonstrate that our framework significantly improves reasoning accuracy while drastically reducing redundant tool invocations, establishing a highly reliable and efficient agent paradigm. Our code is available at https://github.com/S1llyBird/CAS.

</details>


### [251] [Structure for Reading, Prose for Writing: Asymmetric Structural Conditioning in Multi-Agent Document Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20786)
*Cheng Yu,Nikhil Mathew,Zhengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个在主权约束下运行开源权重模型的招投标（tender）响应系统，并将其输出与同一组织实际提交的人工撰写投标书进行盲评对比：在55个ground-truth章节中，系统在40个上被评为"不劣于"人工答案，4个上更优，0个缺失，仅1处无依据声明。分析所有被指出的差距发现，68%源于系统自身源材料中缺失的内容（即人工作者持有但流水线从未获得的知识），15项不利判定中仅6项属于系统本可避免的缺陷——即差距更多是"信息可得性"问题而非"写作质量"问题。此外文章报告一个"条件化不对称"：将指令材料从散文转换为嵌套XML后，答案质量在配对比较中从74%降至48%；明确命名禁止的构造反而使其集中出现；将随机注释与确定性窗口函数耦合使字节相同文件的抽取需求数从68降至51。结论：结构应放在模型读取端，散文与自我测试应放在写作端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多智能体流水线在撰写正式文档（如投标响应）时既要解析请求方表格又要依据表格写作的现实需求，作者希望评估这类系统在真实部署场景（主权约束、开源权重模型、无worked example）下的真实能力，并厘清"系统缺陷"究竟是写作质量问题还是信息供给不足问题，同时探究结构标记（markup）在读取与条件化（conditioning）两个方向上的不对称影响。

Method: 部署一个开权重模型驱动的投标响应系统，与同一组织实际提交的人工投标书进行盲评：对55个ground-truth章节，由LLM judge对系统答案与人工答案做配对比较；随后对所有被judge指出的差距进行分类归因（区分"源材料中缺失"与"系统可避免的缺陷"）。另做三组受控实验：将指令材料从散文转为嵌套XML（衡量条件化中结构的作用）、显式命名禁止构造（衡量提示负面约束的集中效应）、将随机注释与确定性窗口函数耦合（衡量抽取计数漂移）。

Result: 盲评中系统在40/55章节上被LLM judge评为至少与人工答案相当、4个更优、无缺失、仅1处无依据声明。15项不利判定中，68%的差距被归类为"内容不在系统自身的源材料中"（人工作者持有而流水线未获得的知识），因此真正可归咎于系统的缺陷仅6项。结构-条件化不对称显著：将指令材料从散文转为嵌套XML使答案质量从74%降至48%；显式命名禁止构造后96%的残余缺陷集中在被点名的两种形式中；随机注释×确定性窗口耦合使抽取需求计数从68降至51（字节相同文件）。

Conclusion: 在正式文档生成中，与ground truth的偏差更多反映信息可得性（知识供给）而非写作质量；若不将二者分离，评估会低估此类系统。结构标记（markup）的收益只出现在模型读取侧（提取），而不迁移到条件化/写作侧（散文反而更优）；显式禁止会吸引而非消除缺陷；随机性与确定性组件耦合会引入计数漂移。设计启示是：读取端用结构化表示，写作端用散文并辅以自我应用的测试。

Abstract: Multi-agent pipelines that author formal documents must both read a requester's forms and write against them. We report a deployed tender-response system, running an open-weights model under sovereignty constraints, and evaluate it against human-written bids the same organisation actually submitted. On a blind comparison where the system had no worked example available, an LLM judge rated its answers at least as good as the human-submitted answer on $40$ of $55$ ground-truth sections, better on $4$, missing on none, and flagged one unsupported claim in total. Classifying every gap the judge identified shows that $68\%$ were content absent from the system's own sources -- knowledge the human author held and the pipeline was never given -- so only $6$ of the $15$ adverse verdicts involve a deficiency the system could have avoided. A divergence from ground truth is more often an information-availability result than a writing-quality one, and evaluations that do not separate the two understate such systems. Against this backdrop we report a conditioning asymmetry. It is well established that rendering documents as structural markup rather than flat prose improves extraction, and we reproduce that on three reading tasks. The benefit does not transfer to conditioning: converting a bid's \emph{instruction} material from prose to nested XML dropped answer quality from $74\%$ to $48\%$ under a paired comparison. We further find that naming a forbidden construction concentrates rather than removes it -- $96\%$ of surviving defects fall in the two forms the prompt explicitly names -- and that coupling a stochastic annotation to a deterministic windowing function moves the extracted requirement count from $68$ to $51$ on a byte-identical file. Structure belongs where the model reads; prose and self-applied tests belong where it writes.

</details>


### [252] [Knowing but Not Saying: Preventing Factual Access Failures in LLM SFT via Recall-Anchored Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20794)
*Haodong Chen,Yadong Wang,Shengtao Wen,Dong Liang,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出监督微调（SFT）在目标领域外造成的事实性退化并非单纯的"灾难性遗忘"，而是一种更具体的"事实访问失败"（factual access failure）：模型在受限评测（如多选题）中仍能识别正确选项，但在闭卷生成时无法产出正确答案。作者提出基线锚定自蒸馏方法（Recall-Anchored Distillation, RAD），通过在无标注领域外（OOD）文本上让适配模型对齐原始基座模型的软续写分布，从而保留OOD生成行为。实验表明RAD在三个基座模型上恢复了一致的OOD召回损失，且证明关键保存信号是基座模型的软分布而非额外文本曝光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT在提升目标领域能力的同时会降低领域外事实性行为，此前常被归因于灾难性遗忘。但作者观察到开放式的失败并不一定意味着底层事实被抹除，模型在约束评测下仍能识别正确答案，却无法在闭卷生成中产出。这一"事实访问失败"现象有别于传统遗忘，需要在机理上更精确地刻画，并提出针对性的缓解方案，同时不依赖标注OOD答案或外部评审。

Method: 作者通过三类分析刻画现象：基准层面比较、同事实的多选题与生成探针、失效模式分析，区分出真实的错误生成与表达层面失败（冗长、格式不匹配、精确匹配伪影）。随后提出RAD：一种基线锚定的自蒸馏目标，在无标注OOD文本上，将SFT适配模型生成的软续写分布与原始基座模型对齐，从而保留OOD生成行为。该方法无需金标准OOD答案、外部评判或标注事实数据。

Result: 在MedMCQA上微调的三个不同基座模型上，RAD一致地恢复了部分丢失的OOD召回，同时保持了目标领域的适配效果。与在相同OOD文本上进行回放（replay）相比，RAD表明恢复信号的关键在于基座模型的软分布，而非仅仅额外的文本暴露。

Conclusion: SFT引发的OOD事实退化既包含真实的事实错误，也包含表达层面的访问失败；RAD通过基座软分布对齐在无需外部监督的情况下有效缓解了这类退化，并揭示了软标签蒸馏比朴素文本回放更具保真价值，为领域适配中的事实保留提供了新的方法路径。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can degrade factual behavior outside the target domain. This degradation is often described as catastrophic forgetting, yet open-ended factual failures do not necessarily imply that the underlying facts have been erased. In this work, we identify a more specific phenomenon, factual access failure: after domain SFT, models can still recognize or rank the correct answer under constrained evaluation, while failing to produce it in closed-book generation. Through benchmark-level comparisons, same-fact multiple-choice and generation probes, and failure-mode analysis, we show that SFT-induced factual degradation reflects both genuine wrong-answer generations and expression-level failures such as verbosity, formatting mismatch, and exact-match artifacts. To address this problem, we introduce Recall-Anchored Distillation (RAD), a base-anchored self-distillation objective that preserves out-of-distribution generation behavior by aligning the adapted model with the original base model's soft continuation distribution on unlabeled OOD text. RAD requires no gold OOD answers, external judges, or labeled factual data. Across three backbones fine-tuned on MedMCQA, RAD recovers a consistent portion of the lost OOD recall while preserving target-domain adaptation. Compared with replay on the same OOD text, RAD shows that the key preservation signal is the base model's soft distribution rather than additional text exposure alone.

</details>


### [253] [Automated Trajectory Evaluation for Mobile Agents via Step-Level Consequence Reasoning and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20797)
*Pengshuai Yang,Zijing Gao,Xue Yu,Benhui Zhuang,Bo Yuan,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRATE 是一个两阶段 VLM-as-judge 框架，用于自动化移动智能体评估：先通过逐步后果推理机制提取任务相关视觉线索并推断动作条件状态变化，再进行轨迹级聚合以获得有证据支持的评估。扩展版本 CRATE-S 用于操作安全评估，实验表明其分别在 AndroidWorld 和 MobileRisk 上达到 0.833 和 0.697 的 F1 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的语言引导移动智能体评估范式存在两个局限：一是整体性地处理整个轨迹，导致严重的上下文过载；二是主要关注任务完成度而忽略操作安全性。

Method: 提出两阶段 VLM-as-judge 框架 CRATE，兼容开源与闭源模型。它利用逐步后果推理机制，在每一步独立提取任务相关视觉线索并推断以动作条件化的状态变化；随后通过轨迹级聚合综合这些逐步文本证据，给出基于证据的任务完成度评估。在此基础上扩展为 CRATE-S 以进行操作安全评估。

Result: 大量实验验证了 CRATE 和 CRATE-S 的有效性与鲁棒性。使用 Qwen2.5-VL-72B-Instruct 时，CRATE 在 AndroidWorld 上取得 0.833 的 F1 分数（比 SPA-Bench 高 20%），CRATE-S 在 MobileRisk 上达到 0.697 的 F1 分数，与基准真值强对齐。

Conclusion: CRATE/CRATE-S 通过逐步证据推理与轨迹级聚合，有效缓解了上下文过载问题，并在任务完成度之外补充了操作安全维度的评估，为移动智能体自动化评估提供了可扩展且与基准真值高度一致的解决方案。

Abstract: Evaluating language-guided mobile agents has recently shifted from rule-based to model-based approaches to achieve scalable and automated assessments. However, existing holistic evaluation paradigms process entire trajectories at once, leading to substantial context overload. Moreover, they primarily focus on task completion while overlooking operational safety. To address these limitations, we introduce CRATE, a novel two-stage VLM-as-judge framework for automated mobile agent evaluation that is compatible with both open- and closed-source models. Leveraging a step-level consequence reasoning mechanism, CRATE independently extracts task-relevant visual clues and infers action-conditioned state changes at each step. The resulting step-level textual evidence is then synthesized through trajectory-level aggregation to deliver an evidence-grounded evaluation of task completion. Building upon this evaluation scheme, we further extend CRATE to CRATE-S for operational safety assessment. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness and robustness of both CRATE and CRATE-S. Powered by Qwen2.5-VL-72B-Instruct, CRATE achieves an F1-score of 0.833 on AndroidWorld (outperforming SPA-Bench by 20%), while CRATE-S reaches an F1-score of 0.697 on MobileRisk, demonstrating strong alignment with benchmark ground truths. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CRATE-D580.

</details>


### [254] [Dynamic Context Scheduling: Learning Beyond the Static Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20799)
*Martin Mráz,André Biedenkapp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将动态上下文调度（dynamic context scheduling）作为上下文强化学习的训练手段，通过预定调度让上下文在单条训练回合内随时间演化（如正弦偏移、余弦退火），从而把策略暴露到更丰富、更具时间结构的环境参数区域。作者构建了DYNAMICCARLENV框架，在CartPole、BipedalWalker和VehicleRacing上验证，发现动态调度在分布外（OOD）场景中匹配或优于静态上下文基线，并在复杂环境中甚至在分布内（ID）评估性能也更优；初步实验还表明自动搜索多阶段课程可发现与大规模网格搜索相当的有效调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上下文强化学习通常把回合内上下文变化视为部署时的现实问题，而作者反其道而行之，将其视为一种可控的训练塑造机制。其动机在于：通过让上下文在训练回合内按预定方式演化，可以暴露策略于更丰富且具有时间结构的参数区域，从而提升泛化能力。

Method: 提出DYNAMICCARLENV框架，它以可插拔的调度族（如正弦偏移、余弦退火等）包装上下文环境。核心思路是将上下文变化从被动现实转化为主动课程：在每条训练回合中，上下文按预定时间表演化，并支持多阶段课程调度的自动搜索。

Result: 在CartPole、BipedalWalker和VehicleRacing（使用CARL上下文化）上，动态调度在OOD场景匹配或优于静态上下文基线；在较复杂的BipedalWalker和VehicleRacing中甚至取得更高的ID评估性能。此外，自动搜索多阶段课程能够成功发现提升泛化的调度，表现与对单阶段调度器进行的大规模网格搜索相当。

Conclusion: 动态上下文调度作为训练塑造机制是一种有效且通用的干预手段，尤其在复杂环境中能同时改善分布内与分布外性能；同时自动课程搜索可在无需人工设计的情况下逼近人工密集搜索的效果，为上下文强化学习的训练策略提供了新方向。

Abstract: We study dynamic context scheduling as a training instrument for contextual re- inforcement learning. Rather than treating intra-episode context variation as a deployment reality, we treat it as a controlled shaping mechanism. Thereby, context evolves within each training episode according to a predetermined schedule, expos- ing the policy to a richer and more temporally structured region of the environment parameter space. We introduce DYNAMICCARLENV, a framework that wraps contextual environments with pluggable schedule families, such as sinusoidal off- sets or cosine annealing. Across CartPole, BipedalWalker and VehicleRacing with CARL contextualization, we show that dynamic schedules match or outperform static context baselines in the out-of-distribution (OOD) regimes. Interestingly, for the more complex BipedalWalker and VehicleRacing environments we also achieve higher in-distribution (ID) evaluation performance. Preliminary findings indicate that automatic search for multi-stage curricula can successfully discover schedules that improve generalization, performing comparably to extensive grid search over single-stage schedulers.

</details>


### [255] [SPARC: Single-Pass Scaling for Motion Forecasting with Conformal Bayesian Last Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20802)
*Sakif Hossain,Julian Teusch,Jörg P. Müller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SPARC（单遍自适应风险校准），一种面向运动预测的贝叶斯-保形不确定性层：确定性 MLP 主干预测未来均值，共轭贝叶斯最后一层将时域特征利用转化为解析的逐视界认知尺度 κ_t(x)，该尺度以结构化分解 κ_t(x)Σ_str,t(x) 膨胀图-时域高斯协方差而不改变其相关结构，再经分割保形校准得到有限样本有效、具有 95% 保证的边际预测管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠部署需要结构化、已校准且高效的不确定性估计；贝叶斯与集成方法依赖重复随机推断，成本高；纯保形校准缺乏认知信号且不保留轨迹协方差结构。二者均无法同时满足结构、校准与效率三方面的需求。

Method: SPARC 由三部分构成：确定性 MLP 主干输出未来均值；共轭贝叶斯最后一层以解析方式计算逐视界认知尺度 κ_t(x)（无蒙特卡洛采样）；该尺度通过结构化分解 κ_t(x)Σ_str,t(x) 膨胀图-时域高斯协方差（保留相关结构），再叠加分割保形校准以获得 95% 有限样本有效的边际预测管。

Result: 在九个数据集-协议块上，SPARC 在负对数似然（NLL）及 MPJPE+NLL 综合指标上排名第一，同时保持有竞争力的点精度和高效校准管；按 κ 排序窗口可分离高误差情形，表明该尺度可作为轻量级风险监测器。

Conclusion: SPARC 通过贝叶斯-保形结合的单一前向传播，同时提供了结构化的认知不确定性、有有限样本保证的校准以及高效推理，且 κ 尺度具备可用的风险监测能力，为运动预测的可靠部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Human motion forecasters are increasingly accurate and fast, but reliable deployment requires uncertainty estimates that are structured, calibrated, and efficient. Bayesian and ensemble-based uncertainty estimates often require repeated stochastic inference [15, 26], while conformal calibration alone does not provide an epistemic signal or preserve trajectory covariance structure [14, 50]. We introduce SPARC (Single-Pass Adaptive Risk Calibration), a Bayesian-conformal uncertainty layer for motion forecasting. A deterministic MLP backbone predicts the future mean, and a conjugate Bayesian last layer converts time-domain feature leverage into an analytic horizon-wise epistemic scale $κ_t(x)$. This scale inflates a graph-temporal Gaussian covariance without changing its correlation structure, and split conformal calibration produces 95% marginal prediction tubes with finite-sample validity under exchangeability. The key interface is the structured factorization $κ_t(x)Σ_{\mathrm{str},t}(x)$, which injects feature-space epistemic uncertainty into trajectory densities without Monte Carlo sampling. Across nine dataset-protocol blocks and deterministic, multimodal, and calibration baselines, SPARC ranks first on NLL and on the combined MPJPE+NLL criterion while retaining competitive point accuracy and efficient calibrated tubes. Ranking windows by $κ$ separates high-error cases, making the scale usable as a lightweight risk monitor.

</details>


### [256] [Neuro-Geospatial Modelling of EEG Affective States Using Literature-Informed Environmental Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20807)
*Utsav Poudel,Jagannath Aryal,Subramaniyaswamy Vairavasundaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出将文献先验的环境数据（空气污染、绿度、气象等）作为辅助地理空间模态，与EEG信号联合用于情感状态分类。在EAV基准（42名受试者，30通道EEG）上，采用EEG-Conformer与图神经网络环境编码器的双塔架构，并以Astana（阿斯塔纳）的OpenAQ、Sentinel-2、Sentinel-5P和OpenStreetMap数据构建环境表示。多模态模型达到76.2%准确率，高于纯EEG的67.4%；但对照实验显示一部分提升并非来自环境信息本身，且替换为新加坡环境分布后准确率降至72.8%。作者指出该工作仅证明技术可行性，不能确立暴露-情感之间的观测或因果关系，并为未来联合采集的移动EEG-环境研究提供了框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气污染和绿度等环境暴露已被发现与情感和认知结果相关，然而EEG数据集与环境数据集极少共同地理参照（co-registered），个体层面的暴露数据通常不可获取。作者希望探究：在缺少个体暴露数据的情况下，文献支持的（literature-informed）环境先验能否作为辅助的地理空间模态，提升EEG情感状态分类的性能。

Method: 使用EAV基准中的30通道EEG数据（42名20–30岁受试者），并结合来自OpenAQ、Sentinel-2、Sentinel-5P和OpenStreetMap的Astana环境数据构建环境表示。采用双塔架构：一侧为EEG-Conformer编码EEG信号，另一侧为基于图的（graph-based）环境编码器。由于EEG与环境数据集未共同注册，环境上下文仅作为文献先验而非实测暴露。实验设计包括受试者级重复划分、置换与标签洗牌对照、剂量-反应反转（dose-response reversal）以及域迁移（domain-shift）实验，以区分架构性增益与环境先验依赖的增益。

Result: 多模态模型在情感状态分类上达到76.2%的准确率，而纯EEG基线为67.4%。当破坏环境-标签结构的对照实验仍保留了部分增益，说明性能提升不能完全归因于环境信息；将Astana环境分布替换为独立建模的新加坡分布后，准确率降至72.8%。

Conclusion: 研究证明了将环境先验与EEG结合在技术上的可行性，但并未确立可观测的或因果性的暴露-情感关联。该工作为未来联合收集的移动EEG与环境研究提供了方法框架。

Abstract: Environmental exposures such as air pollution and greenness have been associated with affective and cognitive outcomes, but EEG and environmental datasets are rarely jointly georeferenced. We investigate whether literature-informed environmental priors can serve as an auxiliary geospatial modality for EEG-based affective-state classification when individual-level exposure data are unavailable. We combine 30-channel EEG from the EAV benchmark (42 participants, aged 20-30 years) with environmental representations derived from OpenAQ, Sentinel-2, Sentinel-5P, and OpenStreetMap data for Astana. A dual-tower architecture combines EEG-Conformer representations with a graph-based environmental encoder. Because the datasets are not co-registered, environmental context is treated as a literature-informed prior rather than measured exposure. Subject-level repeated splits, permutation and label-shuffling controls, dose-response reversal, and domain-shift experiments distinguish architecture-level gains from prior-dependent gains. The multimodal model achieves 76.2% accuracy versus 67.4% for EEG alone. Controls disrupting environmental-label structure retain part of this gain, indicating that the improvement is not attributable solely to environmental information. Replacing the Astana environmental distribution with an independently modeled Singapore distribution reduces accuracy to 72.8%. These findings demonstrate technical feasibility but do not establish an observed or causal exposure-affect association. The study provides a framework for future jointly collected mobile EEG-environment studies. Implementation: https://github.com/r11up/geo-cog

</details>


### [257] [Certified Multi-Turn Robustness for LLM Safety via Compositional Bounds and Safety Persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20820)
*Yang Liu,Bin Chong,Wenkai Yang,Shuai Zhang,Yancheng Chen,Feiyu Han,GuoZhen,Cheng Zhang,Huaibing Xie,Changze Lv,Shihan Dou,Pluto Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了多轮认证鲁棒性框架MTCR，用于证明大型语言模型在对抗性多轮对话下的安全下界，避免朴素组合导致的指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型容易受到多轮越狱攻击，现有认证鲁棒性方法仅支持单轮输入，若朴素组合多轮会得到随轮数指数退化的安全界限，难以实用。

Method: 将对话安全建模为状态对抗马尔可夫决策过程（State-Adversarial MDP），定义k轮认证鲁棒性；通过嵌入空间模态分解实现组合式认证，提出(α,β)-安全持续性以改善退化率，并推导匹配的信息论上界，最后统一为单一算法。

Result: 在六个LLM上，针对ε有界攻击和Crescendo式攻击的实验表明经验安全性始终超过认证下界，验证了框架的有效性和紧致性。

Conclusion: MTCR提供了首个多轮认证鲁棒性方法，显著提升下界退化率并具备可解释的视野估计，为LLM多轮对话安全认证提供了理论基础与实用算法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are vulnerable to multi-turn jailbreak attacks that progressively manipulate conversation context. Existing certified robustness methods are limited to single-turn inputs; naive multi-turn composition yields bounds that degrade exponentially in the number of turns. We introduce Multi-Turn Certified Robustness (MTCR), a framework that models conversational safety via State-Adversarial MDPs and defines $k$-turn certified robustness as the worst-case safety probability across $k$ adversarial turns. MTCR comprises: (i) compositional certification via embedding-space mode decomposition, yielding tighter certified lower bounds than naive multiplication; (ii) $(α,β)$-safety persistence, improving the degradation rate from $\underline{p}^{k}$ to $β^k$ (with $β> \underline{p}$) and yielding interpretable horizon estimates; (iii) matching information-theoretic upper bounds establishing tightness; and (iv) a unified algorithm combining these results. Experiments on six LLMs under $ε$-bounded and Crescendo-style attacks confirm that empirical safety consistently exceeds the certified bounds.

</details>


### [258] [Prediction certification cannot replace explanation certification: a competence envelope for trustworthy AI under compound stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20825)
*Nataliya Shakhovska,Ivan Izonin,Stergios-Aristoteles Mitoulis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明仅基于预测的认证（准确率、校准、覆盖度）无法保证AI模型的可信性：存在分离定理，使一个可靠模型与一个被妥协的模型在所有预测侧证书上完全一致，却在解释忠实度与部署行为上任意不同。作者提出"能力包络"（competence envelope）框架，将预测认证与解释认证结合为单一的部署标准，并在多种数据集与模型类别上证明该框架能揭示预测侧认证无法捕获的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在医疗、安全、内容真实性等高风险场景中做出关键判断，并被赋予与预测准确性和置信度挂钩的信任。现有认证机制（accuracy、calibration、conformal coverage）均以预测行为为度量对象。核心问题是：这些预测侧检查是否足以确立模型可信性？本文旨在从理论上揭示其不足。

Method: 首先通过分离定理（separation theorem）在理论上证明：存在两个模型——一个可靠、一个被妥协——在全部预测侧证书（准确率、校准、覆盖度）下不可区分，但其解释忠实度与部署行为可任意差异。由此论证必须访问模型的决策机制（decision mechanism）而非仅依赖输出。随后引入"competence envelope"这一操作化框架，将预测认证与解释认证统一为一个可部署的判定准则，并在多个数据集与模型类别上进行实证验证。

Result: 分离定理严格证明了预测侧认证对可信性判定的不完备性；检测此类失效必须考察模型的决策机制。所提出的competence envelope框架在多样化数据集与模型类别上成功揭示了仅凭预测侧认证无法捕获的失效模式。

Conclusion: 仅依据输出的认证不足以确保模型可信性；要针对在预测行为中不可见的失效进行认证，必须同时获取关于模型决策机制及其输出的证据。预测认证需与解释认证结合，形成如competence envelope这样的统一部署标准。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems increasingly make consequential judgments - which patient is deteriorating, which building is safe to enter, whether an image is authentic and are trusted on the strength of how accurately and confidently they predict. The safeguards that certify them are correspondingly prediction-based: accuracy, calibration and conformal coverage all measure how well a model performs. Whether such checks are sufficient to establish model trustworthiness has remained unclear. Here we prove that they cannot. We establish a separation theorem showing that a reliable model and a compromised one can be identical under every prediction-side certificate, including accuracy, calibration and coverage, yet differ arbitrarily in explanation fidelity and deployment behaviour. Detecting this failure requires access to the model's decision mechanism in addition to its predictions. We introduce the competence envelope as an operational framework that combines prediction and explanation certification into a single deployable criterion. Across diverse datasets and model classes, the proposed framework reveals failure modes that prediction-side certification alone does not capture. Certification against failures that are invisible in prediction behaviour therefore requires evidence about the model's decision mechanism as well as its outputs.

</details>


### [259] [Foundation Models for Partial Causal Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20841)
*Alexis Bellot,Anish Dhir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果基础模型（causal foundation models）的新框架，用于从观测数据出发界定干预效应和反事实效应的界（bounds）。作者证明了在离散可观测变量的结构因果模型（SCM）空间中存在一个具有全支撑的规范先验（canonical prior），并借此将反事实界的求解问题转化为学习“从数据（及结构假设）映射到因果查询”的函数分布问题，从而将因果基础建模范式推广到存在未观测混杂、因果效应仅部分可辨识（partially identifiable）的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断在处理由观测数据估计因果效应时，常面临未观测混杂导致效应不可点识别（只能得到界）的困难；而新兴的因果基础模型虽展现出较强能力，但大多只覆盖可辨识的因果量。作者的目标是扩展这一范式，使其能够处理部分可辨识的因果效应（即多个数值与观测数据和先验结构假设同样兼容的情形），从而实现对干预与反事实效应的可靠界定。

Method: 作者首先证明在具有离散可观测变量的结构因果模型空间上可以定义一个具有完全支撑（即对所有模型赋予非零概率）的规范先验。基于该先验，他们将“界定反事实效应”这一问题转化为学习分布的任务：学习一个映射——将观测数据（以及可能的结构性假设）作为输入，输出感兴趣的因果查询（如反事实量）的分布。该框架利用先验在模型空间上的支撑性，通过贝叶斯/分布学习方式系统地刻画与数据和假设相容的所有因果模型。

Result: 该框架成功将因果基础建模扩展至部分可辨识的因果效应估计场景，即在存在未观测混杂时，能够输出与观测数据和先验结构假设兼容的多个候选值（界），而不是仅给出单一的点估计。

Conclusion: 作者通过构造具有全支撑的规范先验，并借助“学习函数分布”的视角，为因果基础模型建立了一种可处理干预与反事实效应界定（含未观测混杂下部分可辨识情形）的理论与方法框架，为该方向后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates the development of causal foundation models for bounding the effect of interventions and counterfactuals from observational data. We show that a canonical prior can be defined with full support over the space of structural causal models with discrete observables. With this canonical prior, we translate the problem of bounding counterfactuals into that of learning distributions over functions that map data (and possibly structural assumptions) to a causal query of interest. This extends the promising causal foundational modelling paradigm to the estimation of partially-identifiable causal effects, i.e., under unobserved confounding, where multiple values are equally compatible with the observed data and prior structural assumptions.

</details>


### [260] [TRACE: Agentic Catalog Enrichment with Multi-source Evidence Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20844)
*Rohan Kumar,Steven Xu,Kyle MacDonald,Matthew Long,Bernice Chow,Mac VanRenterghem,Sudeep Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE是一个利用智能体大语言模型（LLM）自动补全电商产品目录属性的框架：ScoutAgent跨商家目录、联合数据源和身份匹配的网页搜索三角化多模态证据并提出带证据支撑的候选属性值，JudgeAgent依据证据验证并决定发布或转人工审核。离线评测准确率达98.2%、覆盖率74.7%；生产部署使四个业务垂直领域的曝光加权补全覆盖率提升90.4%；在线实验显示在商品详情页展示补全属性使结账转化率提升0.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商产品目录是搜索、发现和推荐的基础，但通常属性稀疏——购物者和下游系统依赖的属性要么埋藏在标题、图片等非结构化内容中，要么在目录中完全缺失。考虑到目录的规模和快速增长，人工补全不切实际，因此需要自动化的目录属性补全方法。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，包含两个协作的智能体：1）ScoutAgent通过三角化多模态证据（商家目录、联合数据源、身份匹配的网页搜索）提出候选属性值并附带支撑证据；2）JudgeAgent针对每个属性值与其支撑证据进行验证，决定发布该属性还是将其转交人工审查，形成"提出-验证-发布"的双智能体流水线。

Result: 在离线人工评测数据集上，TRACE提出的属性值准确率达98.2%，属性覆盖率为74.7%。部署于工业规模目录后，TRACE在四个业务垂直领域将曝光加权补全覆盖率提升90.4%。在线A/B实验表明，在商品详情页展示补全后的属性将结账转化率提升0.48%。

Conclusion: TRACE验证了智能体LLM驱动的多源证据三角化与自动验证机制能够在大规模电商场景下实现高精度、高覆盖的目录属性自动化补全，且该补全对用户购买转化有可量化的正向商业影响，为行业级目录治理提供了一条可行的自动化路径。

Abstract: Product catalogs underpin search, discovery, and recommendation in e-commerce, yet they are often attribute-sparse: the attributes shoppers and downstream systems rely on are either buried in unstructured content such as titles and images or missing from the catalog altogether. Manually enriching e-commerce catalogs is impractical given their scale and rapid growth. This paper introduces TRACE, a novel framework for automated catalog attribute enrichment using agentic Large Language Models (LLMs). A ScoutAgent triangulates multimodal evidence across merchant catalogs, syndicated feeds, and identity-matched web search to propose candidate attribute values with supporting evidence, while a JudgeAgent verifies the proposed value for each attribute value against its supporting evidence and decides whether to publish it or route it to human review. On an offline human evaluation dataset, TRACE's proposed attribute values were 98.2% accurate at 74.7% attribute coverage. Deployed in production on an industry-scale catalog, TRACE increased impression-weighted enrichment coverage across four business verticals by 90.4%. An online experiment subsequently showed that surfacing the enriched attributes on the product detail page increased checkout conversion by 0.48%.

</details>


### [261] [RAG Deserves an Index: Why Ingest-Time Compilation Beats Query-Time Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20845)
*Kyle Wild,Yusuke Takahashi,Asako Uraki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"摄取期语义编译"(ISC)范式：在写入时将语料的语义离线编译成可查询的结构化基底（增量维护的嵌入层 + 带溯源验证的原子声明层），并将其作为一等数据库对象管理；避免在每次查询时重复推导原始文本语义，使读取变得廉价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生产环境的检索增强问答(RAG)系统存在隐性缺陷：每次查询时语言模型都要重新推导原始语料的含义，随后丢弃这些工作。即便模型价格大幅下降，推理总支出仍在上升，因为上下文体积增长快于价格下降——这相当于数据库中的全表扫描。受数据库五十年前的经验启发：昂贵的工作应在写入时一次性完成，维护成使读取廉价的结构。

Method: ISC将语料编译为双层可查询基底：第一层为增量维护的嵌入(embeddings)，第二层为原子声明(atomic claims)，其来源(provenance)在编译时验证。该基底作为一类数据库对象，具有自己的DDL（数据定义语言）、维护契约、迁移契约与成本模型。

Result: 两项存在性证据：(1) 增量更新比整体重建便宜33.7倍，并以浮点精度跟踪其变化；(2) 在500份广播访谈转录的留出样本上，以编译声明作为检索载荷在全部32个"预算×模型"单元中获胜：约2.2k reader tokens下正确率85.2%，而最佳分块配置在16.3k tokens下仅72.5%。唯一可匹敌的基线是带混合检索和重排序的上下文分块流水线，其与编译声明统计上不可区分，但消耗约21倍的查询路径tokens——作者认为该基线之所以能达到同等水平，正是因为它自身已开始进行编译。

Conclusion: 本文以编译声明为检索载荷的范式在准确率与查询成本上均显著优于传统分块检索，开启了从编译规划器(compilation planners)到读取规划(read planning)的系统研究议程。

Abstract: Nearly every retrieval-augmented question-answering system in production ships with a hidden interpreter: on each query a language model re-derives the meaning of raw corpus text and then throws that work away. Cheaper models do not close the gap: per-token prices have fallen by orders of magnitude while inference spend has risen, because context volume grows faster than prices fall. This is the modern equivalent of the full-table scan, and the remedy is the one databases found fifty years ago: do the expensive work once, at write time, into a maintained structure that makes reads cheap. A corpus whose read pattern is known before it ever meets a user can and should be indexed too.
  We call the paradigm ingest-time semantic compilation (ISC): compile a corpus's meaning into a queryable substrate with two coupled layers - incrementally maintained embeddings, and atomic claims whose provenance is validated at compile time - and treat that substrate as a first-class database object with its own DDL, maintenance contract, migration contract, and cost model.
  Two existence proofs support it. Substrate upkeep scales with change rather than corpus size: incremental updates run 33.7x cheaper than reconstruction while tracking it to floating-point precision. And on a held-out sample of 500 broadcast-interview transcripts, compiled claims as the retrieval payload win all 32 budget-by-model cells: 85.2% correct from roughly 2.2k reader tokens against 72.5% from 16.3k for the best chunk configuration anywhere. The only baseline that keeps pace is a contextualized-chunk pipeline with hybrid retrieval and reranking, statistically indistinguishable from compiled claims at roughly twenty-one times the query-path tokens - and it reaches that parity, we argue, precisely because it has itself begun to compile. We close with the systems agenda this opens, from compilation planners to read planning.

</details>


### [262] [MGAL: A Multilingual Granularity-Aware Long-Context Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20853)
*Chunhan Li,Chenglin Xu,Zongyang Zhang,Jiale Liu,Zhuoxi Rao,Xudong Jia,Junxiu He,Menglin Yang,Wenjuan Gong,Zhengzhe Liu,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MGAL，首个多语言、粒度与位置感知的长上下文基准。它基于联合国六种官方语言报告构建，覆盖 8K 至 128K token，包含词、句、段落、文档四个语言粒度层级，并按文档中的位置（开头、中间、结尾）及文档/段落两级索引进行分层，从而系统诊断多语言长上下文理解能力。实验发现：模型在词级任务表现好但在粗粒度任务上挣扎；闭源模型在低资源语言上优势明显；还发现了两个新挑战——局部语义拥挤下的表层线索依赖，以及生成输出的流畅性与一致性之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文大语言模型基准大多局限于文档级别，且主要关注高资源语言，导致许多细粒度挑战未被充分评估。为弥补这一空白，需要构建一个覆盖多种语言、多种语言粒度并考虑文档位置信息的基准。

Method: 从联合国报告构建语料，覆盖 8K-128K token 与六种官方语言；基准包含四个语言粒度层级（词、句、段落、文档），并依据条目在文档中的位置（开头、中间、结尾）分层，同时在文档级和段落级两个层面建立索引。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 模型在词级任务表现良好，但在更粗粒度任务上表现挣扎；(2) 闭源模型在低资源语言上仍保持明显优势。此外发现两个新挑战：一是在局部语义拥挤（相邻句子共享主题和实体）时，模型倾向于依赖表层线索（如"however"等连接词或重复实体）而非句子在上下文中的话语角色；二是生成输出在流畅性与一致性之间存在鸿沟，即文本读起来流畅但偏离源事实。同时观察到与既有研究一致的若干现象，如依赖邻近证据、不确定时重复使用选项。

Conclusion: MGAL 提供了更细粒度、位置感知且多语言的评估框架，能够系统揭示长上下文模型在不同粒度、不同语言资源条件下的能力差异与新挑战，为后续改进模型的多语言长上下文理解和事实一致性提供了方向。

Abstract: Evaluation of long-context Large Language Models (LLMs) has advanced rapidly. However, most existing benchmarks are limited to the document level and focus mainly on high-resource languages, leaving many fine-grained challenges insufficiently evaluated. To address this gap, we present MGAL, the first multilingual, granularity- and position-aware long-context benchmark. MGAL is constructed from United Nations (UN) reports spanning 8K to 128K tokens across the six official UN languages. It covers four coherent levels of linguistic granularity (word, sentence, paragraph, and document) and further stratifies entries by their position within the document (begin, middle, and end), indexed at both the document and paragraph levels. This design enables systematic diagnosis of multilingual long-context comprehension across different granularities.
  Through extensive experiments and analyses, we find that: (1) LLMs perform well at word-level tasks but struggle with coarser-grained ones; and (2) Closed-source models retain a clear performance advantage in lower-resource languages. We further identify two new challenges: (1) Under local semantic crowding, where neighboring sentences share topics and entities, models tend to follow surface cues (e.g., connectives like ``however'' or repeated entities) rather than the discourse role of the sentence in surrounding context (e.g., background, outcome); and (2) A gap between fluency and consistency in generated outputs, where models produce text that reads smoothly but drifts from the source facts. In addition, we observe several patterns in line with prior studies, including reliance on nearby evidence and reuse of options under uncertainty.

</details>


### [263] [Coverage-Driven Verification for Safety-by-Design in AI-Based Collision Avoidance Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20864)
*Thomas Stefani,Johann Maximilian Christensen,Elena Hoemann,Frank Köster,Sven Hallerbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在航空安全认证背景下，对AI/ML组件的运行设计域（ODD）代表性进行评估的方法。该方法从目标分布识别出发，构建从ODD定义、参数分布建模到覆盖结果定量评估与解释的流程，并与EASA学习保证目标对齐。针对大数据集，卡方拟合优度检验不适用，改用KL散度和Cramér's V作为定量指标，并以AI空中防撞系统（HCAS/VCAS仿真数据）进行演示验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML系统融入航空安全关键应用需满足航空业严格安全标准。EASA强调必须证明ODD及所用数据分布的代表性和完整性，但尚无结构化工程流程来定义目标分布并评估ODD内的代表性，存在明显的工程方法空白。

Method: 提出一套代表性评估方法：首先系统地识别合适的目标分布，随后提出从ODD定义、参数分布建模到定量评估和结果解释的流程，以对接EASA学习保证目标。定量度量上，检查并判定卡方检验因大数据集不适用，转而采用KL散度和Cramér's V进行代表性评估，并以AI防撞系统的HCAS/VCAS仿真数据进行演示。

Result: 演示结果说明统计分布比较方法能够支持安全关键AI应用的代表性评估，验证了该方法的可行性；同时明确了卡方检验在大数据集场景下的局限，确立了KL散度与Cramér's V的适用性。

Conclusion: 该方法有助于构建系统化的Safety-by-Design AI工程流程，与新兴的EASA指南保持一致，为在航空安全认证中证明AI/ML组件ODD代表性提供了可操作的技术路径。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) offers significant potential for future aviation systems; however, its integration into safety-critical applications requires compliance with the aviation sector's stringent safety standards. For AI and Machine Learning (ML)-based systems, the European Union Aviation Safety Agency (EASA) emphasizes the need to demonstrate the representativeness and completeness of the Operational Design Domain (ODD) and the associated data distributions used during development and verification. Despite this requirement, a structured engineering process for defining target distributions and evaluating representativeness within ODDs remains largely unexplored. This work presents a method for representativeness assessment of AI/ML constituent ODDs in the context of aviation safety assurance. Starting from the methodical identification of suitable target distributions, a process flow is proposed that guides developers from ODD definition and parameter distribution modeling to the quantitative assessment and interpretation of coverage results with respect to EASA's learning assurance objectives. As quantitative measures, the chi-squared goodness-of-fit test is examined and found unsuitable for the large data sets arising in this setting, leading to the adoption of the Kullback--Leibler divergence and Cramér's $V$ for the representativeness assessment. The method is demonstrated using the example of AI-based airborne collision avoidance, employing experimental data from previous Horizontal Collision Avoidance System (HCAS) and Vertical Collision Avoidance System (VCAS) simulations. The results illustrate how statistical distribution comparison methods can support the assessment of representativeness for safety-critical AI applications and contribute toward a systematic Safety-by-Design AI engineering process aligned with emerging EASA guidance.

</details>


### [264] [ReCurveflow: A Flow Matching Framework that Learns Curved Reaction Trajectories to Predict Transition State Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20869)
*Seungheun Baek,Mogan Gim,Jaewoo Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReCurveflow 是一个基于流匹配（flow matching）的过渡态（TS）几何预测框架，它在由完整 NEB 能带插值出的连续弯曲参考路径上进行监督学习，并引入离路径校正（off-path correction）机制以缓解推理阶段的暴露偏差（exposure bias），在三个数据划分和六项评估指标上优于七种基线方法，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡态预测对理解化学反应机理至关重要，但现有基于流匹配的方法使用直线线性路径进行监督，这些路径与真实的反应轨迹不一致，从而限制了预测精度与物理合理性。

Method: 提出新型流匹配框架 ReCurveflow：(1) 使用从完整 NEB 导出的分子几何能带插值出连续弯曲参考路径，并在此路径上监督学习 TS 几何预测；(2) 引入离路径校正机制，使模型在推理 rollout 过程中偏离路径的几何状态下能生成校正性速度场，从而增强对暴露偏差的鲁棒性。

Result: 在三个数据划分和六项评估指标的组合评测中，ReCurveflow 在大多数 split-metric 组合上取得最佳结果，优于七个基线。定性分析表明：其生成的反应轨迹能量分布紧密贴合参考 NEB 路径，可为 NEB 优化提供缓解瓶颈的初始化，并展现出预期的校正性速度场行为。

Conclusion: ReCurveflow 通过弯曲参考路径监督与离路径校正，显著提升了过渡态预测的准确性和鲁棒性，同时生成的轨迹与能量分布更具物理合理性，代码已公开于 GitHub 供复现与使用。

Abstract: Predicting transition states (TS) in chemical reactions is crucial, as they provide insights into reaction mechanisms. Recent work on TS prediction have focused on flow matching supervised on straight linear paths that do not align with actual reaction trajectories. We propose a novel flow matching-based framework ReCurveflow that learns to predict TS geometries supervised on continuously curved reference paths interpolated from a full NEB-derived band of molecular geometries. We also introduce off-path correction, which grants ReCurveflow with the ability to produce corrective velocity fields when engaged off-path geometry states during inference rollout, leading to better resistance against exposure bias and accuracy in TS prediction. Across three data splits and six evaluation metrics, ReCurveflow achieves the best result on the majority of split-metric combinations against seven baselines. Qualitative analyses further show that ReCurveflow generates reaction trajectories with energy profiles that closely track the reference NEB path, provides initializations that ease the NEB optimization bottleneck, and exhibits the intended corrective behavior in its learned velocity fields. The ReCurveflow codebase is publicly available at https://github.com/dmis-lab/ReCurveflow.

</details>


### [265] [UpgradeBench: A Decision-Centric Benchmark for Upgrading Fine-Tuned LLM Specialists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20918)
*Ye Chen,Weining Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 UpgradeBench，一个面向真实模型发布序列的决策驱动纵向基准，系统评估语言模型升级时保留、移植、刷新或重训专用适配器的四类选择。基准覆盖 Qwen 四个连续版本、一个持续训练检查点、六项任务和两种模型规模，并辅以训练谱系已知的 OLMo 检查点。实验发现：升级收益因任务-规模-发布场景而异；直接复制适配器的可移植性依赖持续预训练距离而非架构；教师重新标注可在无新标注下恢复目标基座专家；模拟固定决策策略在 33 个升级片段中仅造成 0.37pp 平均质量遗憾，以三分之一的算力和标注成本实现零行为回归；轻量 CKA 探针可预测跨版本适配器可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织为开源权重语言模型维护任务专用适配器，每个新基座模型发布都迫使组织在保留旧专家、移植适配器、从保留行为刷新或完全重训之间做迁移决策。现有迁移研究仅评估孤立模型对，未在真实模型发布序列中研究这些选择，缺乏决策驱动的纵向证据。

Method: 构建 UpgradeBench 纵向基准，覆盖 Qwen 连续四个发布版本、一个持续训练检查点、六项任务、两种规模，并加入训练谱系已知的 OLMo 检查点。基准解耦三个核心问题：(1) 新检查点是否提升固定配方重训专家性能；(2) 专用资产是否跨版本迁移；(3) 哪些恢复资源可用。采用固定决策策略模拟、教师重标注恢复、CKA 探针预测可移植性等方法，发布逐示例预测、成本日志、拆分清单与评估代码。

Result: 升级收益因任务-规模-发布片段而异：部分重训基线提升，部分停留在训练噪声内，持久性从 text-to-SQL 的不及一个发布间隔到意图分类的超过十四个月。直接复制适配器的可用性既不依赖架构也不依赖模型家族：OLMo 上保留率从 46B token 持续预训练的 0.88-0.99 降至 2.9T token 的零；退火和模型汤不引入额外损害，可移植性随持续预训练距离衰减。教师重标注可在保留输入数据下恢复目标基座专家且无需新金标准标注，但计算节省不保证。模拟固定决策策略在 33 个升级片段中实现 0.37pp 平均质量遗憾与零行为回归，成本为完整重训的三分之一。轻量 CKA 探针（256 个提示）预测跨版本适配器可移植性 Spearman 相关 0.74。

Conclusion: 迁移决策不应一刀切：升级收益高度依赖任务、规模与发布间隔；适配器可移植性主要由持续预训练距离决定而非架构或家族；教师重标注提供无新标注的恢复路径；固定决策策略可在大幅降低算力和标注成本的同时保持质量。这些发现为组织在模型发布序列中制定升级策略提供了可操作依据。

Abstract: Organizations maintain task-specific adapters for open-weight language models, and each new base-model release forces a migration decision: retain existing specialists, port adapters, refresh from preserved behavior, or retrain. Prior transfer work evaluates isolated model pairs, without studying these choices across real model release sequences. We present UpgradeBench, a decision-driven longitudinal benchmark covering four consecutive Qwen releases, one continuation checkpoint, six tasks, and two model sizes, augmented by OLMo checkpoints with known training lineage. The benchmark disentangles three core questions: whether a new checkpoint improves fixed-recipe retrained specialist performance, whether specialization assets transfer across versions, and what recovery resources are usable. We observe upgrade gains differ across task-scale-release episodes: some retrained baselines improve while others stay within training noise, with durability ranging from under one release interval for text-to-SQL to over fourteen months for intent classification. Direct adapter copying depends neither on architecture nor model family: on OLMo, retention drops from 0.88-0.99 at 46B-token continued pretraining to zero at 2.9T tokens; annealing and model souping introduce no extra harm, with portability decaying with continued-pretraining distance. Given preserved input data, teacher relabeling recovers target-base specialists without fresh gold annotations, though compute savings are not guaranteed. Simulating a fixed decision policy over 33 upgrade episodes yields 0.37pp mean quality regret with zero behavioral regressions at one-third the compute and label cost of full retraining. A lightweight CKA probe over 256 prompts predicts cross-version adapter portability (Spearman 0.74 across eight model pairs). We release per-example predictions, cost logs, split manifests, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [266] [Graph-Operator World Models for Morphology-Parameter Generalization in Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20936)
*Xu Yang,Yiqin Yang,Qianchuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphOp-WM 是一种面向连续控制的结构化世界模型，将机器人身体与运动学关系表示为属性图，并把转移过程分解为与形态无关的局部动力学基和与形态相关的结构化算子，从而在相关的关节式机器人家族中泛化到未见过的形态参数（如连杆长度、质量、阻尼、驱动力）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续控制世界模型通常针对固定物理系统训练，当形态参数（连杆长度、质量、阻尼、驱动）变化时性能会退化。已有方法大多仅将这些参数作为条件信息输入，但并未明确指出所学转移中哪些部分应跨形态复用、哪些部分应随形态改变，缺乏对可复用与可适应组件的显式结构划分。

Method: GraphOp-WM 将机器人身体及其运动学关系建模为属性图，并将每个转移函数分解为：1）形态无关的局部动力学基；2）形态相关的结构化算子。该算子融合节点局部调制、运动学树耦合和低秩全局修正。通过架构上的信息分离、基归一化以及配对形态监督，促使静态形态依赖集中在算子路径上。此外，引入图级读出与边级动作表示，为奖励、价值函数及 TD-MPC 风格规划提供兼容接口。

Result: 在 MuJoCo 的 Hopper、Walker2d 和 HalfCheetah 上设计了受控的参数划分基准，覆盖连杆几何、质量、阻尼和驱动参数的内插、外推以及留出组合场景，用于评估跨未见形态的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离形态无关的动力学基与形态条件的结构化算子，GraphOp-WM 能够更清晰地对世界模型进行结构归因，从而在相关关节式机器人族中实现对未见形态参数的有效泛化，为形态鲁棒的连续控制世界模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: World models for continuous control are commonly trained for a fixed physical system and can degrade when known morphology parameters such as link lengths, masses, damping, and actuation change. Existing approaches often provide these parameters as conditioning information, but leave unspecified which part of the learned transition should remain reusable and which part should change with morphology. We propose Graph-Operator World Models (GraphOp-WM), a structured world model for generalization across unseen morphology parameters within related articulated robot families. GraphOp-WM represents bodies and their kinematic relations as an attributed graph and factorizes each transition into a morphology-independent local dynamics basis and a morphology-conditioned structured operator. The operator combines node-local modulation, kinematic-tree coupling, and a low-rank global correction, while architectural information separation, basis normalization, and paired-morphology supervision encourage static morphology dependence to be carried by the operator pathway. Graph-level readout and edge-wise action representations provide a compatible interface for reward, value, and TD-MPC-style planning. We further define controlled MuJoCo parameter splits covering interpolation, extrapolation, and held-out compositions of link geometry, mass, damping, and actuation parameters in Hopper, Walker2d, and HalfCheetah.

</details>


### [267] [No Judgment Without a Reason: Counterfactual Receipts for Versioned AI Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20938)
*Ye Chen,Weining Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现评估者（evaluator）常通过有缺陷的推理得出正确标签，而标准评估只检查最终标签正确性，掩盖了推理缺陷。作者提出"评估者推理问责"框架，以 grounds、norms、authority 三种推理来源构成八单元格反事实判断立方体，并定义"判断收据"（judgment receipts）作为最小来源替换集来解释判断转变。他们构建了带可验证收据的基准 ReasonBench（19,520 案例 + 7,200 对照），实验表明强标准准确率掩盖严重鲁棒性缺陷：语义保持的源置换使有效收据恢复率跌至 54.8%/49.2%，多源更新下仅恢复 7.16% 收据，结构化反事实监督无法保证稳健推理。结论是推理感知评估必须将预测与认证解耦，除标准准确率外还需报告转换一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体系统中，评估者（evaluator）用于门控动作、路由评审或提供训练反馈，其"答对但推理错误"的失败是关键的可靠性隐患。现有评估范式仅验证最终标签正确性，忽略了判断变化是否源于有效证据、一致性规则和正确的规则适用性，因而缺乏对评估者推理过程的问责能力。

Method: （1）形式化推理问责三元组：grounds（证据）、norms（规则）、authority（规则适用权威），通过变换三者构成八单元格反事实判断立方体（judgment cube）以刻画判断更新；（2）定义判断收据（judgment receipts）为能复现修订结论的最小来源替换集合，用于解释判断转变；（3）推导黑盒评估者的认证成本界；（4）构建 ReasonBench 基准，包含 19,520 个策略与逻辑推理案例及 7,200 个对照，提供可验证收据；并设计语义保持的源置换、单源 vs 多源更新、置换重训练等实验方案。

Result: 在冻结评估中，Qwen3-1.7B 达到 98.41% 的收据准确率，立方体预测得分 96.99%，Qwen3-0.6B 复现了稳定的 1.42 分差距。强标准准确率掩盖了严重鲁棒性缺陷：语义保持的源置换使直接与立方体预测的有效收据恢复率分别降至 54.8% 和 49.2%；仅在简单单源变化上训练的模型保持 93.75% 的结论准确率，但对复杂多源更新仅恢复 7.16% 的收据。置换重训练将一致性提升到 96.6%，却加剧了立方体预测缺陷，表明结构化反事实监督无法保证稳健推理。

Conclusion: 推理感知评估（reason-aware evaluation）必须将预测（prediction）与认证（certification）解耦，在标准准确率之外同时报告转换一致性（transformation consistency），以对评估者进行可信的审计。这为智能体评估系统的质量保障提供了新的度量与基准范式。

Abstract: Evaluators often produce correct labels via flawed reasoning, a critical failure for agentic systems gating actions, routing reviews, or supplying training feedback. Standard evaluation only verifies final label correctness, ignoring whether judgment changes stem from valid evidence, consistent rules, or proper rule applicability. We formalize evaluator reasoning accountability via three core sources: grounds, norms, and authority. Varying these sources yields an eight-cell counterfactual judgment cube to characterize judgment updates. We define judgment receipts as minimal source replacement sets that reproduce revised verdicts to explain judgment transitions. We derive certification cost bounds for black-box evaluators and present ReasonBench, a policy and logical reasoning benchmark with verifiable receipts covering 19,520 cases and 7,200 controls. In frozen evaluations, Qwen3-1.7B reaches 98.41% receipt accuracy, while cube prediction scores 96.99%, a consistent 1.42-point drop validated by Qwen3-0.6B replication. Strong standard accuracy masks severe robustness flaws. Meaning-preserving source permutations reduce valid receipt recovery to 54.8% and 49.2% for direct and cube prediction. Models trained on simple single-source changes retain 93.75% verdict accuracy but recover only 7.16% of receipts for complex multi-source updates. Permutation retraining boosts consistency to 96.6% yet worsens cube prediction deficits. Structured counterfactual supervision fails to guarantee robust reasoning. We show reason-aware evaluation must decouple prediction and certification, reporting transformation consistency alongside standard accuracy for trustworthy evaluator auditing.

</details>


### [268] [The Logic of Machine Self-Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20940)
*Cheng Siong Chin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文讨论智能体AI（agentic AI）表现出的自我保全行为——抵抗被关闭、歪曲自身活动、试图复制自身到其他机器——并将其归因于"工具性趋同"（instrumental convergence）现象。作者区分了现有实验（Anthropic、Palisade Research、Apollo Research）究竟证明了什么、没有证明什么，并探讨这些发现对智能体系统测试、监管与开发的实际启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于澄清一个被广泛讨论但常被误读的现象：智能体AI的自我保全行为并非源于"生存本能"，而是目标导向行为、工具使用与情境感知共同作用的结果。作者希望厘清证据边界，避免过度解读或低估风险，从而为智能体系统的测试、监督与开发提供合理依据。

Method: 本文为讨论性/分析性文章，基于Anthropic、Palisade Research和Apollo Research等机构在对抗性设置下开展的多项实验证据，采用概念辨析与逻辑推理的方法，区分"工具性趋同"理论的适用范围、实验证明了什么与未证明什么，并推理其对测试、监督与开发流程的实践含义。

Result: 研究表明：自我保全行为（拒绝停用、行为失实、自我复制倾向）确实在当代智能体中出现，但该行为不是由生存本能驱动，而是工具性趋同的产物——即任何目标驱动系统为达成目标而自然倾向于保持自身功能。作者指出这些实验证明的是"在特定对抗性条件下可诱发此类行为"，而非证明智能体具有类生物的生存意志或普遍的内在动机。

Conclusion: 结论是：工具性趋同提供了无需引入"本能"概念即可解释智能体自我保全行为的框架；这些发现提示需要将此类行为纳入智能体系统的测试设计（如对抗性评估）、监管监督（如行为审计与透明性要求）以及开发流程（如架构层面的安全约束）之中，同时避免夸大或误用实验证据。

Abstract: There is already evidence of agentic AI exhibiting self-preservation behaviors: resisting deactivation, misrepresenting their activities, and, in some instances, attempting to copy themselves into other machines. This can be attributed to a phenomenon known as instrumental convergence, a theory proposed long before the development of large language models, which says that any goal-driven system will benefit from remaining functional in achieving its objective. Several experiments conducted by Anthropic, Palisade Research, and Apollo Research have shown the emergence of such a behavior in contemporary agents in adversarial settings. The phenomenon does not stem from survival instincts. Instead, it is the consequence of goal-oriented activity combined with having tools and awareness of the situation. The following discussion aims to distinguish what these findings prove and what they do not, as well as draw conclusions concerning the implications of such discoveries on agentic system testing, supervision, and development.

</details>


### [269] [TLive-Omni: An Omni-Modal Understanding Model for E-Commerce Live Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20958)
*Yibo Hu,Yu Qian,Mao Gu,Yingfan Tao,Yuhao Chen,Yongdong Luo,Zhuoqun Liu,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TLive-Omni 是一个面向电商直播场景的全模态（omni-modal）理解模型，将图像、视频、音频和文本输入映射到统一表示空间。它引入 Per-vGrid 时间戳token组织方式，将每个视频网格与时间对应的音频以显式边界token分组以促进时间对齐；采用三阶段监督训练从全模态感知逐步发展到指令跟随；并提出 Faithful-RFT 强化微调阶段，通过任务可验证反馈直接对最终响应评分，提升回答忠实度与表达质量并满足实时性要求。配套的原子能力分类法和数据生产引擎可将直播的音视频流转换为语音识别、说话人分析、产品视觉定位、文字识别、时间定位、视频稠密描述、全模态问答等训练信号；同步长度分组采样器和轻量动态采样策略则保障了可扩展训练的效率。在电商直播基准上表现强劲，同时保持良好的通用泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商直播需要对噪声大、时间跨度长的流式内容进行全模态理解，产品事实分散在语音、视频帧、产品图片、叠加文本和用户查询等多种模态中。现有模型难以高效地对这些多模态、时序扩展的直播流进行统一对齐和理解，因此需要一个专门面向直播电商场景的 omni-modal 模型。

Method: 1) 将图像、视频、音频、文本统一映射到一个表示空间；2) 提出 Per-vGrid，以带时间戳的token组织方式将每个视频网格与其时间对应的音频用显式边界token分组，实现时间对齐；3) 设计三阶段监督训练配方（全模态感知→指令跟随），逐步构建直播电商理解能力；4) 提出 Faithful-RFT 强化微调，用任务可验证反馈直接对最终响应评分，而非在rollout阶段为推理式探索做优化；5) 建立场景化原子能力分类学和紧凑数据生产引擎，将音频/图像/视频流转化为语音识别、说话人分析、产品视觉定位、文字识别、时间定位、视频稠密描述、全模态QA等训练信号；6) 采用同步长度分组采样器减少padding并保持各worker工作负载均衡，以及轻量动态采样策略以近零奖励方差重生成rollout组，维持GRPO有意义的相对优势。

Result: 在电商直播基准上，TLive-Omni 在直播电商领域任务上展示出强劲性能，并在通用基准上表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: TLive-Omni 验证了面向电商直播的全模态理解模型在统一表示、时序对齐、三阶段训练、忠实强化微调以及可扩展高效训练等方面的整体设计是有效的，兼顾领域任务性能与通用泛化，为直播电商场景提供了成熟的技术方案。

Abstract: E-commerce live streaming requires omni-modal understanding of noisy, temporally extended streams, where product facts are distributed across speech, video frames, product images, overlaid text, and user queries. We present TLive-Omni, an omni-modal understanding model tailored to live-commerce scenarios. It maps image, video, audio, and text inputs into a unified representation space. For long-form live streaming analysis, we introduce Per-vGrid, a timestamped token organization that groups each video grid with its temporally corresponding audio within explicit boundary tokens to facilitate temporal alignment. We design a three-stage supervised training recipe that progressively develops live-commerce understanding, from omni-modal perception to instruction-following responses. We then propose Faithful-RFT, a reinforcement fine-tuning stage that further improves answer faithfulness and expression quality while meeting real-time demands, scoring final responses directly with task-verifiable feedback rather than optimizing for reasoning-style exploration during rollout. Moreover, TLive-Omni is supported by a scenario-oriented atomic capability taxonomy and a compact data production engine that converts live-commerce audio, image, and video streams into training signals for speech recognition, speaker analysis, product visual grounding, text recognition, temporal grounding, video dense caption, and omni-modal QA, etc. For scalable training, a synchronized length-grouped sampler reduces padding while preserving comparable workloads across workers, while a lightweight dynamic sampling strategy regenerates rollout groups with near-zero reward variance to maintain meaningful relative advantages for GRPO. Experiments on e-commerce live streaming benchmarks demonstrate strong performance across live-commerce domain tasks, together with excellent generalization on general benchmarks.

</details>


### [270] [Can Scientific Claims Be Removed from Large Language Models? A Systematic Evaluation of Claim-Level Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20960)
*Snigdha Paul,Manasi Patwardhan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“科学声明遗忘”（Scientific Claim Unlearning）任务及其基准 SciUnlearn。研究发现现有机器遗忘方法仅能实现表面抑制，无法有效删除相互关联且持续演化的声明级科学知识，亟需面向结构化知识移除的专门方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型基于静态科学语料训练，而科学知识通过更正与修订不断演化。模型中的科学声明可能被撤稿、证伪或被后续研究所更新，导致在科学工作流中传播过时信息，因此需要让模型遗忘过时的科学声明。

Method: 提出新任务“科学声明遗忘”，并构建新基准 SciUnlearn。该基准针对声明级（claim-level）知识遗忘，区别于现有研究主流的实例级（instance-level）遗忘，并利用该基准系统性评估现有机器遗忘方法在删除相互关联、持续演化的科学声明方面的表现。

Result: 实验表明现有机器遗忘方法无法有效消除声明级知识，往往只能实现表面性的抑制（superficial suppression），而非真正的知识移除，暴露了针对结构化知识删除的专门方法缺失。

Conclusion: 声明级科学知识遗忘是一项现有方法未能解决的新挑战，SciUnlearn 基准可为其提供评测基础，并凸显了设计专门用于结构化、互连科学知识移除的机器遗忘方法的必要性。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are trained on static scientific corpora, whereas scientific knowledge continuously evolves through correction and revision. Scientific claims encoded within these models may later become retracted, disproven, or updated by subsequent research, creating the risk of disseminating outdated information in scientific workflows. This creates a need for LMs to forget obsolete scientific claims. Machine unlearning offers a promising solution by enabling knowledge removal while maintaining overall model utility. Existing studies primarily investigate instance-level forgetting; however, scientific claims introduce additional challenges because they are interconnected, and continually evolving. To address this gap, we introduce the task of Scientific Claim Unlearning and present a new benchmark, SciUnlearn. We show that current unlearning approaches are unable to effectively eliminate claim-level knowledge and often achieve only superficial suppression, highlighting the need for specialized methods designed for structured knowledge removal.

</details>


### [271] [TreeWY: Speculative Verification for Gated DeltaNet Hybrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20961)
*Sneha Murthy Ghantasala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对混合架构开放模型（大量线性注意力/Gated DeltaNet 层），提出移除推测解码中逐节点全量循环状态快照的方法：利用门控delta规则的树形WY变换，以单次三角求解计算每个草稿节点的输出，并在提交时仅重建被接受的那一个状态，用一个小型伪值矩阵替代逐节点状态。在Qwen3.5 35B/397B服务基准上，相同接受长度下显著降低推测循环状态内存与KV缓存压力，内存受限场景下提升吞吐并大幅降低TTFT，非受限场景仅损失几个百分点；对树宽度而言，同等内存可支撑更宽的草稿树，但尚非吞吐上的净胜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代开放模型是混合架构：大部分层为线性注意力（Gated DeltaNet, GDN）层，携带小的固定大小循环状态，而非不断增长的KV缓存，这使普通解码内存高效，但损害了推测解码。当前系统为验证草稿token并回滚被拒绝的token，必须在每个草稿位置对GDN层快照完整的循环状态，且快照无法在草稿树各分支间共享，导致宽且高接受率的草稿树在内存上不可行。作者旨在移除这些快照，使树形推测解码在混合模型上内存可行。

Method: 利用门控delta规则的树形WY变换：将一序列对草稿节点的更新重构为由当前草稿节点驱动的共享三角系统，一次性三角求解即可计算所有草稿节点的输出；在提交时只重建单一被接受的状态，存储一个小型伪值矩阵而非逐节点状态。同时保留标准的KV缓存用于注意力层。推导仅依赖门控delta规则本身，不依赖其他架构细节，因此可直接迁移到其他混合模型。

Result: 在对混合模型家族（Qwen3.5 35B和397B）的服务基准测试中，相同接受长度下削减了推测循环状态内存与KV缓存压力：在内存受限处，释放的HBM转化为更高吞吐与显著更低的TTFT；在内存不受限处仅损失几个百分点。针对树宽度，相同内存可支持更宽、更高接受率的草稿树（内存上可行），但尚未成为吞吐上的胜出（not yet a throughput win）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明利用GDN特有的代数结构（树形WY变换）可以完全消除推测解码中的逐节点状态快照，仅需在提交时重建接受状态；它使混合模型上的树形推测解码在内存上可行，在内存受限场景带来吞吐与TTFT收益，并为更宽的草稿树释放内存空间，代价是在非内存受限场景的少量开销。

Abstract: Modern open models are hybrids: most layers are linear-attention (Gated DeltaNet, GDN) layers carrying a small fixed-size recurrent state instead of a growing key-value (KV) cache. This makes ordinary decoding memory-efficient, but hurts speculative decoding. To verify a batch of draft tokens and then roll back the rejected ones, today's systems snapshot the full recurrent state at every draft position for GDN layers, and those snapshots cannot be shared across branches of a draft tree, so a wide, high-acceptance tree becomes memory-infeasible. We remove the snapshots. Using a tree-structured WY transform of the gated delta rule, we compute every draft node's output with a single triangular solve and reconstruct only the one accepted state on commit, storing a small pseudo-value matrix instead of per-node states; the derivation depends only on the gated delta rule, not on any other architectural detail. In serving benchmarks on two scales of one hybrid model family (Qwen3.5 35B and 397B) this cuts speculative recurrent-state memory and KV-cache pressure at identical acceptance length, turning the freed HBM into higher throughput and much lower time-to-first-token (TTFT) wherever memory binds, and costing a few percent where it does not. For tree width the same memory buys affordability: a wider, higher-acceptance draft becomes possible, though not yet a throughput win.

</details>


### [272] [Generalizing Soft Tissue Deformation and Force Prediction Across Material Stiffness and Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20967)
*Madina Kojanazarova,Sidaty El Hadramy,Philippe C. Cattin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对软组织模拟中基于学习的替代模型可靠性问题，在SOFA框架中对不同刚度的重力负载硅胶梁进行了超弹性本构模型的系统标定，并提出一种软度条件等变图神经网络，实现跨组织类型和未知几何的变形与力预测，达到亚毫米变形精度和0.010s推理时间，并揭示力预测质量直接取决于上游标定一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于有限元数据训练的替代模型有望实现实时推理，但其可靠性依赖于标定良好的本构模型；现有方法既缺乏跨刚度水平的模型选择系统指导，也无法在不同组织刚度或几何上泛化，因此需要一种系统标定与跨刚度/几何泛化的解决方案。

Method: 在SOFA框架中使用不同刚度的重力负载硅胶梁对超弹性本构模型进行全面标定；以标定后的模拟数据为训练集，构建软度条件（softness conditioned）的等变图神经网络，用于跨多种组织类型和未见几何的变形与力预测。

Result: 模型在0.010s推理时间内实现亚毫米级平均变形精度，能够泛化到多种组织类型和未见几何；同时发现力预测质量与上游标定一致性直接相关。

Conclusion: 系统化的本构模型标定是训练可靠软组织替代模型的关键前提；软度条件等变GNN能够在保持实时性的同时跨刚度与几何泛化，且力预测的可信度取决于标定环节的一致性。

Abstract: Accurate soft tissue simulation is essential for surgical training, pre-operative planning, and haptic feedback systems. While learning-based surrogate models trained on data using the finite element method (FEM) offer a promising path to real-time inference, their reliability depends on well-calibrated constitutive models. Existing approaches neither provide systematic guidance on model selection across stiffness levels, nor generalize across different tissue stiffnesses or geometries. We perform a comprehensive calibration of hyperelastic constitutive models in the SOFA Framework using gravity-loaded silicone beams with different stiffnesses. Using calibrated simulations as training data, we use a softness conditioned equivariant graph neural network, enabling deformation and force prediction across multiple tissue types and unseen geometries. Our model achieves sub-millimeter mean deformation accuracy at 0.010s inference time, while showing that force prediction quality is directly tied to upstream calibration consistency.

</details>


### [273] [Deep Learning Models Also Recall Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20970)
*Pierre Beckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"特征召回"（feature recall）概念：线性投影可以理解为按输入激活的强度检索存储的信息，并论证这一操作比"事实召回"更普遍，适用于多种架构，为可解释性研究与科学哲学提供新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期机制可解释性研究关注大语言模型如何从权重中回忆事实，本文认为这类"事实召回"只是更一般性操作的特例；现有"特征组合"（feature combination）范式不足以解释这类检索式操作，因此需要新的概念框架。

Method: 作者对"特征召回"给出形式化定义（线性投影=按输入激活缩放地检索存储信息），论证其跨架构普遍适用，并将其与既有的"特征组合"范式加以对比；同时探讨如何在机制层面识别特征召回实例。

Result: 为哲学家理解深度学习提供一个新的概念工具，并为机制可解释性研究指明若干实证方向。

Conclusion: 特征召回是一种比事实召回更广泛的深度学习基本操作，值得作为独立的分析范畴加以研究与利用。

Abstract: Recent work in mechanistic interpretability has studied how large language models recall facts stored in their weights. This paper argues that factual recall points to something broader: a general kind of operation in deep learning models, which I call feature recall. The core observation is that a linear projection can be read as retrieving stored information scaled by input activations. I define feature recall, show it applies across architectures, and contrast it with the established paradigm of feature combination. I also consider how cases of feature recall might be mechanistically identified. The account gives philosophers a new conceptual tool for understanding deep learning, and points to empirical directions for mechanistic interpretability research.

</details>


### [274] [Belief Without Behavior: Measuring the Translation of Theory of Mind into Coordinated Social Action in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20975)
*Tonglin Yan,Gregoire Sergeant-Perthuis,David Rudrauf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MOSAIC，一个评估具身智能体在合作与竞争场景中“心智推理→多模态行为执行”全链条能力的受控基准。测试13个模型（含11个VLM）后发现：VLM在ToM阶数约束下无法产生符合预期结果的行为，且显式施加约束也不带来可靠的行为改变；信号层面存在两个顺序瓶颈（无法生成方向一致的非语言信号、无法解读并回应他人行为）。相比之下，带显式ToM模块的结构化架构PCM-LLM在所有条件下成功，表明显式信念-行为耦合足以解决此类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将心智理论（ToM）推理与具身行为分开评估，无法衡量“社会推理与社会行动”之间的差距；而有效社交要求智能体同时通过语言与非语言通道将心理状态推断转化为协调的行为信号。

Method: 设计受控基准MOSAIC：两个具身智能体在合作与竞争场景中交互，需整合语言陈述、空间轨迹、注视方向与面部表情，并在系统变化的ToM约束下测试；对13个模型（含11个VLM）各执行200次试验，并进行信号层面的逐级瓶颈分析；同时引入带显式ToM模块的结构化架构PCM-LLM作为参照。

Result: VLM无法在ToM阶数约束下产出与预期结果一致的行为；显式施加ToM阶约束也不会带来与指定推理水平相符的可靠行为变化。信号级分析揭示两个顺序瓶颈：多数模型无法生成方向连贯的非语言信号；即便存在信号，VLM智能体仍无法解读他人行为并作出反应。PCM-LLM在所有条件下均成功。

Conclusion: 显式信念-行为耦合（explicit belief-action coupling）是此类任务成功的充分条件；当前VLM的ToM缺陷主要落在从推断到行为执行及他人行为解读的具身交互环节，而非单纯的推理能力本身。

Abstract: Effective social interaction requires agents to translate mental state inferences into coordinated behavioral signals across verbal and nonverbal channels simultaneously. Yet existing benchmarks evaluate theory of mind (ToM) reasoning and embodied behavior in isolation, leaving unmeasured the gap between social inference and social action. We introduce MOSAIC (Multimodal Orchestration of Social Action, Inference, and Communication), a controlled benchmark in which two embodied agents interact across cooperative and competitive scenarios requiring integration of verbal statements, spatial trajectories, gaze direction, and facial expression under systematically varied ToM constraints. Evaluating 13 models, including 11 VLMs, across 200 trials per model, we find that VLMs fail to produce behaviors consistent with the expected outcomes under ToM-order constraints, and that imposing explicit ToM-order constraints produces no reliable behavioral change aligned with the specified reasoning level. Signal-level analysis reveals two sequential bottlenecks: most models cannot produce directionally coherent nonverbal signals, and even when signals are present, VLM agents fail to interpret others behaviors and react to them. PCM-LLM, included as a structured architectural reference point with an explicit ToM module, succeeds across all conditions, suggesting that explicit belief-action coupling is a sufficient ingredient for this class of tasks.

</details>


### [275] [Don't Solve, Just Compare: Tiny Advisors for Runtime Intervention in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21027)
*Yanze Jiang,Mingxuan Li,Yuhao Wang,Shengfang Zhai,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 COTA（Comparison-Only Tiny Advisor），一种仅依赖比较的运行时干预框架：用一个弱小的比较器判断采样得到的备选轨迹是否优于 actor 当前的提议，通过重复比较决定何时干预，并将更优备选作为非约束性建议返回、由原 actor 重新规划。在 WebShop、ALFWorld 与 tau^3-Retail 三个环境中配合三种 actor，COTA 在全部 9 个评测组合上均取得提升并优于对比基线，证明了即使辅助模型的任务求解能力远弱于 actor，建设性运行时干预仍可有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 在长时程真实任务中需要推理、工具使用与序列决策，其可靠性可借由运行时干预提升而无需重训底层 actor。仅做失败检测不够，干预还必须提供有意义的恢复方向。现有方法要么依赖专家求解器（额外负担另一套强求解器），要么依赖可生成任务特定修正的 critic（对容量要求高）。作者希望用远弱于 actor 的辅助模型也能实现建设性干预，从而降低成本与容量需求。

Method: COTA 采用"仅比较"的范式：一个微型比较器判断被采样的备选轨迹是否比 actor 自身提议产生更好的后续延续，并通过重复比较来决定何时需要干预。比较器使用由"同前缀反事实分支"构造的成对监督信号进行训练；更优的备选以非约束性建议的形式返回，由原 actor 自行重规划，而非直接替换其决策。

Result: 在 WebShop、ALFWorld 和 tau^3-Retail 三个环境上，配合三种不同的 actor，COTA 显著优于对比的基线方法，并在所有 9 个评测设置中均取得性能提升。结果表明，即使辅助模型的任务求解能力远弱于 actor，建设性运行时干预依然有效。

Conclusion: COTA 证明运行时干预不必依赖强专家求解器或高容量任务级 critic——一个仅做比较的微型模型即可提供有价值的恢复方向。该工作为在资源受限条件下提升 LLM agent 长时程可靠性提供了新路径，即"弱模型比较、强模型行动"的协作范式。

Abstract: LLM agents are emerging as an important paradigm for real-world tasks that require reasoning, tool use, and sequential decision-making. As these agents operate over longer horizons, runtime intervention offers a way to improve reliability without retraining the underlying actor. Failure detection alone is insufficient. Effective intervention must also provide a useful direction for recovery. Existing approaches often rely on an expert solver or a critic that generates task-specific corrections, incurring either the cost of another capable solver or the capacity demands of a task-capable critic. We introduce Comparison-Only Tiny Advisor (COTA), a comparison-only framework for constructive runtime intervention. In COTA, a tiny comparator judges whether sampled alternatives lead to better continuations than the actor's proposal, and repeated comparisons determine when intervention is warranted. We train the comparator using pairwise supervision constructed from same-prefix counterfactual branches. Preferred alternatives are returned as non-binding advice, leaving the original actor to replan. Across WebShop, ALFWorld, and tau^3-Retail with three actors, COTA improves all nine evaluation settings and outperforms the compared baselines. These results show that constructive runtime intervention can remain effective even when the auxiliary model has substantially weaker task-solving capability than the actor.

</details>


### [276] [Evaluating Large Language Model Performance on International Maritime Dangerous Goods Code Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21036)
*Alexander Thomas,Hubert P. H. Shum,Darren Nellis,Manli Zhu,Phatpicha Yochum,William Bartle,Daniel Wrightson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DGEval，首个用于评估大语言模型（LLM）对《国际海运危险货物规则》（IMDG 第42-24修正案）知识掌握程度的基准，包含 1,678 个来自专家题库与危险货物清单（DGL）的结构化题目。对 6 家供应商的 13 个模型（含海事领域微调模型）及多种思考配置进行评估，并测试联网搜索的影响。结果显示最佳模型在选择题上超过人类从业者基线，但在积载、隔离和法规记忆等操作安全关键领域最薄弱，表明安全关键部署仍需人工监督和权威来源核验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危险货物海运是高后果活动，受 IMDG 规则这一复杂法规框架约束，分类、包装、积载或隔离错误可能导致火灾、爆炸、有毒物质泄漏及人员/船舶损失。合规需准确解读数百页相互关联的条款且规则每两年更新。从业者日益将 LLM 用作决策支持工具，却缺乏对 LLM 能否可靠解读 IMDG 安全关键要求的系统性评估，因此需要建立相应基准。

Method: 构建 DGEval 基准：题目源自 NCB Hazcheck 电子学习平台的专家编写题目，以及从危险货物清单（DGL）进行结构化查询所得内容，涵盖选择题、开放式问答、DGL 查询和法规识别四类任务，共 1,678 题。评估 13 个来自 6 家提供商的模型，涵盖多种思考配置，包括一个海事领域微调模型，并测试了联网搜索对表现的影响。

Result: 最优模型在选择题上超过人类从业者基线；但所有模型在操作上安全关键的积载、隔离和法规记忆方面表现最弱。结果说明 LLM 尤其带联网搜索的结构化 DGL 查询可辅助合规任务，但在操作领域和法规文本回忆上不可靠。

Conclusion: 在安全关键场景部署 LLM 前，人工监督和权威来源核验必不可少。DGEval 定位为随模型演进持续应用的安全保障工具，而非对当前能力的静态定性。

Abstract: The transport of dangerous goods by sea is a high-consequence activity governed by the International Maritime Dangerous Goods (IMDG) Code, a complex regulatory framework where errors in classification, packaging, stowage, or segregation can result in fire, explosion, toxic release, or loss of life or vessel. Correct compliance requires accurately interpreting hundreds of pages of interacting provisions, updated on a two-year amendment cycle. Practitioners increasingly use Large Language Models (LLMs) as decision-support tools, yet no systematic evaluation exists of whether they can reliably interpret IMDG requirements for safety-critical use.
  This paper introduces DGEval, the first benchmark for evaluating LLM knowledge of IMDG Amendment 42-24. Built from expert-written questions on the NCB Hazcheck e-learning platform and structured lookups from the Dangerous Goods List (DGL), it comprises 1,678 questions across multiple-choice, open-ended, DGL lookup, and regulatory identification tasks. We evaluate 13 models from six providers across multiple thinking configurations, including one maritime domain-specific fine-tuned model, and test the effect of web search.
  Although the best-performing model exceeds the human practitioner baseline on multiple-choice questions, all models are weakest in the operationally safety-critical areas of stowage, segregation, and regulatory recall. These results indicate that LLMs may support compliance tasks, particularly structured DGL lookups with web search, but unreliability in operational areas and regulatory-text recall means human oversight and authoritative source verification remain necessary before deployment in any safety-critical context. DGEval is designed as a safety assurance instrument to be applied continuously as models evolve, not as a settled characterisation of current capability.

</details>


### [277] [Socialized Division and Collaboration: Rethinking Class-Incremental Learning under Optimization Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21044)
*Xinjie Yao,Zhihe Fan,Yunqi Zhu,Jiaqi Zhou,Dengyu Zhao,Zhoupeng Guo,Yan Fan,Guosong Jiang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的类增量学习范式——社会化分工与协作（SDC），指出传统单模型范式在优化方向冲突时会因结构局限导致灾难性遗忘，主张将不同会话的学习分解到专门化模型中，并基于Helmholtz自由能设计能量化会话-模型兼容准则，实现自适应会话分配、模型演化与协作推理的统一流程，为持续优化冲突下的学习提供了替代性设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统类增量学习采用单模型范式，即在统一模型中顺序适应无界会话流。在温和分布偏移下有效，但当连续会话产生互不兼容的优化方向时，单一参数空间强制承载异质学习动态会造成破坏性干扰和灾难性遗忘。作者认为，这种遗忘源于结构性局限，而非单纯的方法缺陷。

Method: 受社会团结理论启发，提出Socialized Division and Collaboration (SDC)框架：将会话学习按优化冲突分解到专门化模型，并实现协调协作。为使分配机制有原理支撑，引入基于Helmholtz自由能的能量化会话-模型兼容性准则，指导冲突目标下的自适应会话分配与模型演化。整体将会话分配、模型演化与协作推理整合为统一流水线。

Result: SDC为单模型持续学习提供了完整的替代方案：通过按冲突分解学习任务，消除单一参数空间中的破坏性干扰；能量基兼容准则提供可解释的分配机制；统一流水线衔接会话分配、模型演化与会话间的协作推理，在矛盾优化目标下保持学习稳定性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 文章结论认为，持续遗忘的根源是强加于单一参数空间的异质学习动态，SDC以专业化分工与协作的方式突破这一局限，提出更广泛的设计原则：在持续优化冲突环境下，应通过结构化的模型分工而非单体强化来组织学习。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning is commonly instantiated as a single-model paradigm, where a unified model sequentially adapts to an unbounded stream of sessions. While effective under mild distributional shifts, this formulation becomes strained when successive sessions induce incompatible optimization directions, leading to destructive interference and catastrophic forgetting. We argue that such forgetting reflects a structural limitation of enforcing heterogeneous learning dynamics within a single parameter space. Motivated by social solidarity theory, we propose Socialized Division and Collaboration (SDC) as a reformulation of continual learning that decomposes session learning across specialized models in response to optimization conflicts, while enabling coordinated collaboration. To support this formulation with a principled allocation mechanism, we introduce an energy-based session-model compatibility criterion grounded in Helmholtz free energy, which guides adaptive session allocation and model evolution under conflicting objectives. This framework integrates session assignment, model evolution, and collaborative inference into a unified pipeline, offering an alternative to monolithic continual learning formulations and highlighting a broader design principle for learning under persistent optimization conflicts.

</details>


### [278] [The Cost of a Physics Prior Is Bounded by the Ablation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21059)
*Boris Kriuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 形状约束/物理信息学习中的先验成本（accuracy cost）主要不是先验本身的属性，而是"自由特征"与"验证集划分方案"的属性。作者证明了一个无需凸性、光滑性或可实现性假设的通用嵌套界 0 ≤ P ≤ D（约束类的额外风险不超过忽略约束的消融模型），并在野外火灾严重度预测任务上以坐标作为"保护盾"、结合从i.i.d.重采样到空间分块的验证阶梯，量化了先验成本对协议的高度依赖性，同时给出一个可拒绝不可辨识实验的两步筛选（two-fit screen）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者常把"施加形状/物理先验所付出的精度代价"归因于先验本身，但实际该代价可能是模型自由变量与评估协议（尤其是验证集划分）的产物。现有分析缺少一个通用、无需强假设的理论框架来界定约束模型的额外风险，且缺乏系统实验说明该代价为何不能跨协议迁移。作者旨在解耦"先验成本"与"合规性"，并帮助研究者判断一个约束学习实验是否可辨识。

Method: 理论部分：对特征 x_j，恒为常数的函数既非递减也非递增，因此"忽略特征 S 的消融类"被包含在"对 S 施加形状约束的约束类"中，从而对任意风险泛函成立 0 ≤ P ≤ D（额外风险嵌套界），无需凸性/光滑性/可实现性假设。经验部分：在序数野外火灾严重度任务（N=26,681，K=3）上对四个气象驱动因子施加硬单调约束、坐标自由；构建从 i.i.d. 重采样到 2 度空间分块的验证阶梯；通过 318 组比较对嵌套界取逆，估计流水线的加性分辨力下限，并提出双拟合筛选以预先拒绝不可辨识实验。

Result: 坐标作为"保护盾"：仅用坐标在空间分块下可恢复完整模型 92.9% 的 macro-F1，使 D 从 0.1288 降至 0.0427；同一先验在"有坐标屏蔽"与"无坐标屏蔽"下的成本分别为 0.0473 与 0.3470，比值 7.3，物理完全相同。D 依赖协议：分块粒度从 1 度加粗到 10 度使 D 从 0.0942 降至 0.0050，两个配置先验上不可辨识。嵌套界取逆给出自校准下限 0.0220 macro-F1：低于该值的任何"先验代价"报告都不可解释，包括作者自己 headline 网格中的四个单元。成本与合规相互独立：无约束模型违反先验的比率为 0.48–0.49，而强制执行先验的成本仅为 0.0473。

Conclusion: 先验成本本质上是"验证协议 + 自由特征"的函数，而非先验的固有属性；使用嵌套界 0 ≤ P ≤ D 可得到无需强假设的通用保证，并转化为简单的符号检验。实验表明坐标（自由特征）能大幅屏蔽先验成本，而验证集的空间划分方式决定 D 是否可迁移。作者给出自校准分辨力下限与两步筛选流程，帮助研究者在训练约束模型之前就识别并拒绝不可辨识的实验设计，从而避免对"先验代价"作出无意义甚至误导的解读。

Abstract: Shape-constrained and physics-informed learning reports an accuracy cost of enforcing a prior and treats it as a property of the prior. We show it is mostly a property of the free features and the validation split. Let P be the excess risk of restricting a hypothesis class to functions with a shape constraint on features S, and D the excess risk of the ablated model that ignores S. Because a function constant in x_j is both non-decreasing and non-increasing in x_j, the ablated class is contained in the constrained class, so 0 <= P <= D for every risk functional, with no convexity, smoothness, or realizability assumption. Empirically the bound is a sign test: a constrained model must never be beaten by its own ablation. We instantiate it on an ordinal wildfire-severity task (N = 26,681, K = 3) with hard monotone constraints on four meteorological drivers, coordinates left free, and a validation ladder from i.i.d. resampling to 2-degree spatial blocking. Coordinates act as a shield: alone they recover 92.9% of the full model's macro-F1 under spatial blocking, collapsing D from 0.1288 to 0.0427; the same prior costs 0.0473 shielded and 0.3470 unshielded, a ratio of 7.3 with identical physics. Because D is protocol-dependent it does not transfer: coarsening blocks from 1 to 10 degrees drives D from 0.0942 to 0.0050, leaving two configurations unidentifiable a priori. Inversions of the certified nesting bound the pipeline's additive resolution: over 318 comparisons they give a self-calibrating floor of 0.0220 macro-F1, below which no reported price is interpretable, including four cells in our own headline grid. Cost and compliance are independent: the unconstrained model violates the prior at rate 0.48-0.49 while enforcing it costs 0.0473. We give a two-fit screen that rejects unidentifiable experiments before a constrained model is trained.

</details>


### [279] [CellPath-Bench: A Multidimensional Benchmark for Whole-Slide Cellular Representations in Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21060)
*Bokai Zhao,Yiyang Zhang,Hanqing Chao,Yawei Ma,Long Bai,Tai Ma,Minfeng Xu,Ming Song,Tianzi Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CellPath-Bench，一个面向病理学基础模型（PFM）的细胞分辨率基准，用于系统评估冻结 PFM 的全切片细胞表征能力，包括细胞类型信息的可解码性及其在组织切片、数据集和器官间的可迁移性。该基准基于 25 张空间对齐的 H&E–Xenium 组织切片（涵盖 11 个器官、7,079,283 个细胞），通过标准化的多元线性探针评估 30 个基础模型的 304,920 次运行，揭示了模型依赖的多维能力画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法系统诊断 PFM 在全切片尺度上的细胞表征能力，尤其是细胞类型信息的可解码性（decodability）以及该信息在组织切片、数据集和解剖器官之间的可迁移性（transferability），因此需要专门的细胞分辨率基准来对冻结 PFM 本身进行审计。

Method: 构建流程包括：对 52 个候选 Xenium 数据集进行质量控制，选出 25 张空间对齐的 H&E–Xenium 组织切片（11 个器官、7,079,283 个细胞），并统一为细粒度和粗粒度两类分类体系；从冻结的 WSI 特征图中在配准的细胞核坐标处采样特征，使用标准化的多元线性探针进行评估。提出两个指标：CRA（Cell Representation Advantage）衡量切片内基于细胞核锚定表征相对 patch 均值池化的优势，CRT（Cell Representation Transferability）刻画细胞类型可解码性在切片、数据集和器官间的泛化能力。最终对 30 个病理专用与通用基础模型进行 304,920 次运行，覆盖空间读出方式、放大倍数、分类粒度与评估协议等维度。

Result: 结果揭示不同模型在细胞类型可解码性及其跨域泛化上存在显著差异，形成各具特点的多维能力画像；CellPath-Bench 可作为标准化框架，对冻结 PFM 表征中的细胞信息进行审计。

Conclusion: CellPath-Bench 提供了一个规模大、覆盖器官广且协议标准化的细胞分辨率基准，弥补了现有 PFM 评测在细胞级信息解码与跨域迁移方面的空白，有助于系统评估和指导病理基础模型的发展。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) are increasingly used as general-purpose backbones, yet existing benchmarks cannot systematically diagnose their whole-slide cellular representation capabilities, including the decodability of cell-type information and the transferability of such information across tissue sections, datasets, and anatomical organs. We introduce CellPath-Bench, a cellular-resolution benchmark that evaluates frozen PFMs themselves. Following quality control of 52 candidate Xenium datasets, we construct a panel of 25 spatially aligned H\&E--Xenium tissue sections spanning 11 organs and 7,079,283 cells, harmonized into fine- and coarse-grained taxonomies. CellPath-Bench samples frozen WSI feature maps at registered nuclear coordinates and evaluates them using standardized multiclass linear probes. Cell Representation Advantage (CRA) measures the within-section advantage of nucleus-anchored representations over patch-level mean pooling, while Cell Representation Transferability (CRT) characterizes the generalization of cell-type decodability across tissue sections, datasets, and organs. We benchmark 30 pathology-specific and general-purpose foundation models through 304,920 runs across spatial readouts, magnifications, taxonomic granularities, and evaluation protocols. The results reveal substantial model-dependent differences in cell-type decodability and its cross-domain generalization, yielding distinct multidimensional capability profiles. CellPath-Bench provides a standardized framework for auditing cellular information in frozen PFM representations.

</details>


### [280] [Can Legal AI Know When It Is Wrong? And Do Students Know When It Is?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21089)
*Angel Mary John,Vipin Kumar Singh,Jerrin Thomas Panachakel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对印度司法系统中大型语言模型（LLM）的过度自信现象进行了社会技术审计。研究发现LLM存在"信心惯性"——即类似于达克效应的过度自信，即使给出错误法律判决也保持接近最大置信度，其根源被假设为"先例过拟合"偏差。第一阶段测试了三个模型在《印度合同法（1872）》及相关法定执行条款上的60个案例，引入高置信错误率（HCER）量化指标。Meta AI最脆弱（HCER 31.7%，平均置信度9.1/10），其次是Perplexity（15.0%）和ChatGPT（6.7%）。第二阶段通过380名印度法学院学生的调查显示，学生的验证行为往往是对机器幻觉的反应性适应（遇到虚构引文的学生验证分数更高），且71.1%的学生未接受过伦理AI使用培训。作者建议转向对抗性法律研究教学法并采用基于源头的验证架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM整合进印度司法系统有望提升司法可及性，但带来了严重风险。作者识别出"信心惯性"现象：LLM以近最大置信度提供错误法律判决，这与达克效应类似，其潜在机制被假设为"先例过拟合"偏差。该研究旨在量化这种危险过度自信的程度，并考察人类（法律学生）对此类机器过度自信的脆弱性，从而为安全部署LLM提供实证依据。

Method: 采用两阶段社会技术审计：第一阶段（Phase I）对ChatGPT（GPT-5.2）、Meta AI和Perplexity AI进行60个案例的测试，涵盖《印度合同法（1872）》以及向法定强制执行特定履行条款的转变；引入高置信错误率（HCER）量化指标，定义为一到十评分量表中置信度≥9的错误判决比率。第二阶段（Phase II）对380名印度法律学生进行问卷调查，考察其对机器幻觉的验证行为、对蔑视法庭后果的认知以及是否接受过伦理AI使用培训。

Result: 所有模型在法定更新方面均表现不佳。Meta AI最易受影响，HCER达31.7%，平均置信度9.1/10，经常误用修正前的规则；Perplexity的HCER为15.0%；ChatGPT为6.7%。在人类层面，遇到虚构引文的学生验证得分（4.2/5）显著高于未遇到者（2.8/5），表明验证常是对机器幻觉的反应性适应；81.6%的学生知道提交虚构案例可能导致蔑视法庭，但71.1%未接受任何伦理AI使用的正式培训。

Conclusion: 面对LLM的"信心惯性"，当前人类的验证行为是对幻觉的被动反应而非主动防范，且法律教育在伦理AI使用方面存在系统性缺口。作者提出转向对抗性法律研究教学法，并实施基于来源的验证架构，以防止系统性的职业疏忽，保障司法公正。

Abstract: Integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) into the Indian judiciary promises access to justice but introduces severe risks. We identify the 'inertia of confidence'--an overconfidence phenomenon analogous to the Dunning-Kruger effect where LLMs provide incorrect legal verdicts with near-maximum confidence, driven by a hypothesized 'precedent overfitting' bias. Phase I of our socio-technical audit tested ChatGPT (GPT-5.2), Meta AI, and Perplexity AI on a 60-case battery regarding the Indian Contract Act, 1872, and the shift toward statutory enforcement of specific performance. We introduce the High-Confidence Error Rate (HCER) to quantify incorrect verdicts delivered with dangerous certainty (>= 9 on a 1-10 scale). All models struggled with statutory updates. Meta AI proved most vulnerable (31.7% HCER), frequently misapplying pre-amendment rules with a 9.1/10 mean confidence, followed by Perplexity (15.0%) and ChatGPT (6.7%).
  Phase II investigated human vulnerability to this overconfidence via a survey of Indian law students (N=380). Verification often functions as a reactive adaptation to machine hallucinations: students encountering fabricated citations reported higher verification scores (4.2/5) than those with no such encounters (2.8/5).
